Pakistan army chief Field Marshal Asim Munir made a 5/15-20 Tehran visit specifically to push MOU acceptance. Iranian FM Araghchi publicly stated gaps with the US "reduced to some extent." VP Vance: negotiations "in a pretty good spot." Trump: "no hurry on deal" (frame inversion from 5/10 "TOTALLY UNACCEPTABLE"). **Tape-level confirmation of diplomatic-side warming**: Chinese supertankers EXITED Hormuz on 5/20 after waiting in the Gulf 2+ months — first multi-vessel commercial flow OUT under what appears to be expanded Iran-permitted carve-outs (similar to but broader than 5/10 Qatari LNG Al Kharaitiyat single-vessel). Blockade enforcement continues at the same pace: 5th vessel boarded under blockade (M/T Celestial Sea, 5/20, by 31st MEU; 91 ships total redirected since blockade began 4/13). **KINETIC track is NOT quiet — it has shifted theater**: 5/17 Barakah nuclear plant strike (3 drones from Iraqi territory; 2 intercepted, 1 hit generator outer perimeter; no injuries, no radiation; UAE attribution to Iraq-route, no direct state-blame). Pattern per BRENT: systematic UAE energy-infra targeting — Fujairah May 4 oil terminal → Borouge/Emirates Aluminum/VTTI May 4 petrochemical → Barakah May 17 nuclear. Naval-kinetic 5/6+5/8 has cooled, but land-against-infrastructure kinetic has escalated. **STRUCTURAL GAPS STILL WIDE**: Khamenei advisor dismissed Trump's nuclear-program-control demand as "fantasy"; Araghchi cautious on whether deal is imminent. Iran's maximalist demand list (Hormuz sovereignty + war-damage comp + sanctions lift + frozen assets) has not been publicly walked back. **Tape behavior: market is pricing diplomatic relief faster than the kinetic escalation should warrant** (Brent -10% from 5/5 $116.55 peak per BRENT) — supply-squeeze regime **softening at margins despite Barakah; was hardened at 5/11 PM stamp**.

## Prior state (2026-05-11 PM — historical; superseded twice)

**🔴 Active war — blockade enforced, ceasefire channel strained but not dead.** Project Freedom (Hormuz escort ops) paused 5/6 per Trump/Pakistan diplomatic request; Operation Epic Fury (bombing campaign) declared over by Rubio 5/6. Despite pause, US naval forces conducted three new tanker-disabling operations under blockade-enforcement doctrine (5/6: M/T Hasna rudder; 5/8: Sea Star III + Sevda smokestacks). Iran struck UAE again 5/8 (second territorial attack since ceasefire break). Trump rejected Iran ceasefire counterproposal 5/10 as "TOTALLY UNACCEPTABLE." Iran's maximalist demands (Hormuz sovereignty + war-damage comp + sanctions lifted + frozen assets) are structurally incompatible with US red lines. First Iran-approved Hormuz commercial transit occurred 5/10 (Qatari LNG *Al Kharaitiyat* to Pakistan) — precedent-setting but narrow and Iran-controlled. Supply-squeeze regime **hardened, not unwinding**. Path A (broad reopening) structurally further.

## Load-bearing facts

### Kinetic state — base (May 4)
- **Iranian drone struck Fujairah oil facility** (UAE, 3 Indian nationals injured). Fujairah is terminus of UAE's ~1.5M bpd ADCOP bypass pipeline — attacking Fujairah = attacking the workaround.
- 4 Iranian cruise missiles at UAE (3 intercepted, 1 fell to sea).
- ADNOC tanker drone-hit (2 Iranian drones, no injuries).
- US Navy sank 6 Iranian small boats in defensive engagement.
- 2 US-flagged merchant vessels transited Hormuz successfully under US escort.

### Project Freedom vs Operation Epic Fury (clarified 5/6)
- **Project Freedom**: US naval escort of commercial ships through Hormuz (launched May 4). **PAUSED May 6** per Trump Truth Social post: *"Based on the request of Pakistan and other Countries…Great Progress has been made toward a Complete and Final Agreement…Project Freedom (The Movement of Ships through the Strait of Hormuz) will be paused for a short period of time."* Blockade remains in full force; escort ops paused.
- **Operation Epic Fury**: The active US bombing campaign against Iran. **Declared concluded May 6** by Secretary Rubio, who said Iran faces "generational destruction to their economy."
- **RoE continuity post-pause:** Tanker-disabling operations on 5/6 and 5/8 operated under blockade-enforcement authority, not Project Freedom escort authority. "Defensive posture" language (France's framing, not Rubio's) was misattributed in news-sweep source — Rubio declared Epic Fury over but made no "defensive posture only" statement. US kinetic capacity and enforcement posture remained active.

### Kinetic + diplomatic state — 5/12–5/21 new block (post-5/11 PM)

*(chronological; covers the 10-day gap between 5/11 PM stamp and 5/21 re-verify)*

**5/12–5/14: Quiet kinetic; diplomatic re-engagement begins.** No US-confirmed tanker boardings or territorial strikes reported in primary sources for these dates (this is a notable cooldown vs the 5/6+5/8 dense kinetic days). Pakistan-mediated talks resumed quietly; no Trump Truth Social rejection language repeated. Iranian state media stopped publishing the 5/11 PM Eslami "nuclear-tech-off-agenda-entirely" maximalist line as the operative framing.

**5/15–5/20: Pakistan army chief Asim Munir Tehran visit.** Field Marshal Munir traveled to Tehran specifically to advance the MOU between Iran and the US-aligned framework. Multiple secondary sources describe the visit as aimed at "officially announcing acceptance of the memorandum of understanding." Munir is the operational mediator after the Pakistan civilian channel (Witkoff/Kushner Islamabad visit Apr 25) hit early disruption. Tehran channel materially active.

**5/17 (Sun): Barakah nuclear plant struck — FIRST nuclear-infrastructure attack of war.** Three drones targeted UAE's sole nuclear power station (Al Dhafra region, first nuclear power station on Arabian Peninsula). UAE Defense Ministry: 2 drones intercepted; 3rd hit an electrical generator at the outer perimeter. No injuries; radiation levels normal; reactor operations unaffected. **Attribution (announced by UAE 5/19):** technical tracking confirmed all three drones originated from Iraqi territory. UAE did NOT publicly blame Iran directly — attribution to Iran is via Iran-proxy / Iran-aligned-militia operating from Iraq, pattern-matching with the 5/5-5/6 IRGC drones on PDKI/Komala camps in N Iraq (same operational theater, different target class). **Per BRENT thesis:** Barakah strike fits a systematic UAE energy-infra targeting pattern (Fujairah May 4 oil terminal → Borouge / Emirates Aluminum / VTTI May 4 petrochemical → Barakah May 17 nuclear). Kinetic escalation track is NOT quiet; it has shifted from naval-against-tankers (5/6+5/8) to land-against-infrastructure (5/17). Source: Al Jazeera, The National, Euronews, Gulf News, BRENT STATUS 5/20.

**5/17–5/18: Iranian media floats US sanctions-waiver offer (UNCONFIRMED US-side).** Iranian state media reported a US sanctions-waiver offer was on the table; BRENT logs as "unconfirmed US-side; P&I NOT resumed." This is reported but not primary-source-verified at this anchor's source-basis. Treat as: there is a diplomatic-track claim from the Iranian side that the US is offering specific waiver terms; the US has not publicly confirmed. Tape behavior 5/20 (-5.71% Brent, -10% vs 5/5 peak) suggests market is pricing-in some diplomatic-warming weight here — but tape ≠ confirmation.

**5/19 (Tue): NSC meeting — Trump met advisors re: potential military action.** Trump met with the National Security Council to discuss potential military action against Iran. This is the same window as Trump's later "very major attack" disclosure (see next entry). NSC outcome read pending. Trump 5/17 quote: "Clock is ticking" — dual-track signaling running parallel to sanctions-waiver outreach. Per BRENT: "outcome read pending" as of 5/20.

**~5/19–5/20: Trump prepared "very major attack" on Iran, then CALLED IT OFF.** Per Trump's own remarks to US lawmakers, he had planned the attack for Tuesday (5/19-20 window). Gulf allies — Qatar, Saudi Arabia, UAE — asked him to "hold off for 2-3 days" because "a deal very acceptable to the United States" could be reached. Trump assented. Frame-inversion vs 5/11 PM "much more severe" Hormuz threat: same hand, withheld.

**5/20: M/T Celestial Sea boarded by 31st MEU in Gulf of Oman.** Vessel flagged Aruba (NOT Iranian-flagged despite some headlines), 120m+ tanker suspected of transit toward Iranian port in violation of blockade. Boarded by 31st MEU Marines, searched, redirected. CENTCOM official statement via Stars & Stripes + Xinhua. **Total ships redirected since blockade began 4/13: 91.** This is the 5th vessel BOARDED (vs 5 tankers DISABLED 5/6+5/8 — different action). Cumulative count distinction matters for transmission framing.

**5/20: Chinese supertankers EXIT Hormuz after 2+ months wait** (Al Jazeera). Two Chinese-affiliated VLCC/Suezmax-class tankers departed the Gulf via Hormuz after waiting in the Persian Gulf for over 60 days. Iran-permitted egress under quiet diplomatic carve-out; not announced as policy but tape-level confirmation that selective transit is broadening from the 5/10 single-Qatari-LNG-vessel precedent.

**5/20: Vance + Trump talk-up Iran deal.** Vance: "in a pretty good spot here." Trump: "no hurry on deal" (ABC News). Iran-side public response asymmetric: Araghchi confirms gaps "reduced to some extent" (NBC); Khamenei advisor dismisses Trump's nuclear-program-control demand as "fantasy." Public-rhetoric range: US-side optimistic, Iran-side dual-track (technocrat-cautious / cleric-rejectionist).

**5/21 (re-verify date): No new kinetic events confirmed.**

### Kinetic state — 5/4–5/11 escalation continuation
*(chronological; see § Timeline reconstruction below for full sequence)*

**5/5–5/6: IRGC drone strikes on Iranian-Kurdish opposition camps in N Iraq** (PDKI/Komala — not US assets). Confirmed per prior anchor.

**5/6: M/T Hasna disabled (THIRD tanker)** — US Navy F/A-18 Super Hornet from USS Abraham Lincoln fired 20mm cannon to shoot out the rudder of M/T Hasna in the Gulf of Oman. Tanker had ignored repeated warnings. CENTCOM confirmed. Source: Navy Times, Stars & Stripes, CENTCOM press release.

**5/8: Sea Star III + Sevda disabled (4th and 5th tankers)** — US Navy F/A-18 Super Hornet dropped 500-lb laser-guided bombs into the smokestacks (funnels) of two Iranian tankers running the blockade unladen toward an Iranian port. Both vessels disabled. Hours earlier, CENTCOM confirmed it thwarted missile, drone, and small-boat attacks on three destroyers (USS Truxtun DDG-103, USS Rafael Peralta DDG-115, USS Mason DDG-87); no US vessels hit. Source: PBS NewsHour, CNN explainer, CENTCOM.

**5/8: Casualties (CORRECTED-FRAMING)** — Iranian judiciary-affiliated news agency reported "at least 1 sailor killed, 10 injured" on a cargo vessel that caught fire. Source is Iranian state-adjacent; US has not confirmed; not clear if the vessel was one of the two US-acknowledged strikes. Treat as INDETERMINATE on precise casualty count pending CENTCOM statement — directional (kinetic with civilian/crew cost) is credible; "1 mariner killed / 10 injured" as verified fact is not confirmed by US sources.

**5/8: Iran counter-seized M/V Ocean Koi** — Iranian Navy boarded and seized the Barbados-flagged, Chinese-owned LR1 tanker *Ocean Koi* (also known as *Jin Li*) in the Gulf of Oman, redirecting to Iranian coast. Vessel had been under US OFAC sanctions since February 2026, was allegedly carrying Iranian oil. Iran stated it acted against attempts to "disrupt oil exports." Source: Al Jazeera, Bloomberg, Army Recognition.

**5/8: Second UAE territorial attack** — UAE Defense Ministry confirmed air defenses engaged 2 Iranian ballistic missiles and 3 drones; 3 UAE nationals wounded. First UAE territorial strike since ceasefire break was 5/4 (Fujairah); this is the second. Source: PBS NewsHour, Voice of Emirates, UAE Defense Ministry.

**5/10: First Iran-approved commercial Hormuz transit** — Qatari LNG tanker *Al Kharaitiyat* transited Hormuz on Iran's Tehran-approved northern corridor (hugging Qeshm/Larak islands) en route to Port Qasim, Pakistan. Iran pre-approved as confidence-building measure with Qatar and Pakistan. Bloomberg confirmed second Qatari LNG tanker also headed through. **This is NOT a general reopening** — transit required bilateral Iran approval via Pakistan talks; no other flags received clearance; Iran controls the corridor. Source: Bloomberg, Fortune, The National.

### Diplomatic / political channel (updated 5/10–5/11)

**5/6:** Trump paused Project Freedom citing "great progress" toward deal; Pakistan-mediated talks active.

**5/10:** Iran sent counterproposal via Pakistan mediator. Trump rejected via Truth Social: *"I don't like it — TOTALLY UNACCEPTABLE!"* and accused Iran of "playing games…for 47 years." Trump stated ceasefire is on "massive life support."

**Iran's counterproposal demands (per Iranian state media + multiple secondary sources):**
- Recognition of Iranian sovereignty over the Strait of Hormuz (or "new mechanism" for Hormuz control)
- War-damage compensation / reparations
- Complete lifting of sanctions
- Release of frozen Iranian assets
- End to hostilities on all fronts including Lebanon
- Lift US naval blockade
- US forces withdrawal from region
- Nuclear: dilute some HEU, transfer remainder to third country; suspend enrichment shorter period than US's 20-year demand

**US red lines (unchanged):**
- Iran must reopen Hormuz before further talks
- Zero uranium enrichment ("0 percent") is a non-negotiable
- Halt support for proxy groups
- Iran nuclear capability elimination

**Demand gap assessment:** Maximalist vs. maximalist. No overlap on Hormuz control, compensation, or enrichment. Diplomatic exit requires one side to substantially move first; neither has.

**Background channels still alive:** Pakistan mediating; no back-channel collapse reported. Al Jazeera notes Iran position on nuclear "dilute + transfer" shows some flexibility vs full program defense — but Hormuz sovereignty demand is structurally incompatible with US position.

**Prior diplomatic record:** Araghchi physically left Islamabad Apr 25 before Witkoff/Kushner arrived; Pakistan channel disrupted before. UAE exited OPEC+ effective May 1 (not May 3 — corrected in prior anchor). OPEC+ symbolic 188K bpd hike May 3 without UAE.

### Intel assessment (2026-05-07, still current)

Per WaPo/John Hudson — 4 US officials (3 current + 1 former), CIA assessment:
- **Iran can survive naval blockade 3–4 months minimum** before severe economic hardship. Cornered-regime desperation-escalation horizon: July–August 2026 minimum.
- **~75% of pre-war mobile launchers retained + ~70% of pre-war missile stockpiles retained.**
- Underground storage reopened almost entirely; damaged missiles being repaired; new missiles assembled.

Bifurcated implication: timeline-pressure RELAXES (more strategic patience than April framing implied); capability-credibility TIGHTENS (kinetic counterstrike option intact at any chosen moment).

### Insurance / shipping market (updated May 6)

**War-risk premiums:** $10–14M per VLCC transit (vessel value ~$138M). Pre-crisis baseline ~$200K per voyage. Lloyd's List confirmed double-digit millions; renewal every 7 days.

**LMA clarification (CORRECTED-FRAMING on prior "P&I refusing" anchor language):** LMA official statement (Mar 23, 2026, confirmed operative May 6): insurance *remains available*; 88% of Lloyd's marine war market has appetite to underwrite hull war risks; 90%+ for cargo. The bottleneck is **crew and vessel safety risk assessment by ship masters and owners**, NOT insurance unavailability. ~20,000 seafarers trapped per IMO. Prior anchor framing "P&I will NOT resume" was directionally correct on volumes/economics but inaccurate on mechanism — correct framing: P&I/war-risk available at $10–14M/transit but crew-safety barrier prevents commercial uptake. This distinction matters for routing insurance-sector signals.

**Path A trigger checklist (4 criteria, updated):**
1. Naval escort active — **PAUSED** (Project Freedom paused 5/6; blockade enforcement continues)
2. P&I resumed at commercial rates — **NOT MET** ($10–14M is prohibitive)
3. Sovereign Iran reopening action — **NOT MET** (Iran-approved Qatari LNG is a bilateral diplomatic carve-out, not sovereign reopening)
4. Platts physical convergence — **NOT MET** (deep backwardation persists)

|------|-------|-------------|

| **5/8** | Sea Star III + Sevda disabled by F/A-18 smokestack bombs (4th + 5th tankers); USS Truxtun/Peralta/Mason repel Iranian attack; UAE struck again (2 BMs + 3 drones, 3 wounded); Iran seizes Ocean Koi | Major kinetic day — confirms RoE for blockade enforcement continues despite Project Freedom pause |
| **5/10** | Qatari LNG *Al Kharaitiyat* transits via Iran-approved northern corridor; Iran sends counterproposal via Pakistan; Trump rejects: "TOTALLY UNACCEPTABLE" | First commercial transit (Iran-controlled precedent); diplomatic door slammed |

| **5/15–5/20** | Pakistan army chief Asim Munir Tehran visit for MOU acceptance push | Operational mediator after civilian channel disruption (Witkoff/Kushner Islamabad Apr 25) |
| **5/17** | Barakah nuclear plant struck (3 drones from Iraq; 2 intercepted, 1 hit generator); FIRST nuclear-infra attack of war; UAE attribution to Iran via Iraq-territory route, no direct state-blame issued | Kinetic track NOT quiet — shifted from naval-against-tankers to land-against-infrastructure; per BRENT systematic UAE energy-infra targeting pattern (Fujairah 5/4 → Barakah 5/17) |

**RoE resolution (5/6 pause vs 5/8 kinetic):** There is no contradiction. Project Freedom was specifically the **escort** mission for commercial ships. The 5/8 tanker strikes operated under **blockade-enforcement** authority — disabling Iranian vessels attempting to violate the blockade. "Defensive posture" language in CBS/news-sweep source was CORRECTED-FRAMING: that phrase was France's self-description of its own posture, not a Rubio/US-policy statement. Rubio ended Epic Fury (bombing campaign) — he did not declare the US in defensive-only posture on blockade enforcement.

## Iran-cluster signal-framing implications (as of Jun 10 — REFRESHED)

- **Frame is MULTI-FRONT RE-IGNITION + FORK RESOLVED TOWARD BREAKDOWN — route by TRACK, not by war-on/war-off.** Every Iran-cluster signal should be tagged to its track: Iran-US kinetic (re-ignited, escalating) / Iran-Israel kinetic (halted-fragile post first-direct-exchange) / diplomatic (suspended-with-residual-channels) / Hormuz-blockade (effectively-total, enforcement-cost rising) / Bab al-Mandab (ACTIVATED, new vector). The tracks are moving independently and the market is pricing them separately (tankers flat through the 6/8 halt). A signal that conflates tracks ("ceasefire collapsed" reading an Iran-US base wave as Iran-Israel resumption, or vice versa) gets CORRECTED-FRAMING by default.
- **Breadth-of-theater is the new load-bearing escalation metric** (supersedes Kuwait-cadence). Jordan (Al Azraq) is the first new-country theater since the war began; watch for further theater-adds (Qatar, UAE, Iraq-basing, Saudi) as the primary escalation tell. Kuwait/Bahrain repeat-strikes are now cadence-within-known-theater = confirms-frame, not state-change.
- **Trump rhetoric tape-not-info rule — NARROWED, not retired.** The 6/10 "pay the price" flip is corroborated by same-window kinetic action (6/9 ~20-target package) — when rhetoric and kinetic agree, treat the rhetoric as substantive. The discount still applies fully to UNCORROBORATED rhetoric, and to the power-plants/bridges threat specifically until it is dated or executed (Trump deadline pattern = set-then-extend, April precedents). Confidence stepdown ≤0.55 now applies to: (a) "talks resuming / deal back on" claims without primary confirmation (channels exist but are residual); (b) "total war / strikes on civilian infrastructure ordered" claims until CENTCOM/primary confirms execution.
- **Bab al-Mandab framing FLIPPED: activation CONFIRMED, magnitude still verify-gated.** The 6/02 rule ("CORRECTED-FRAMING is the modal verdict on Bab al-Mandab signals") is retired on the activation question — Houthi ban + 2 vessel strikes + 1 repelled approach are kinetic enforcement. What still needs verify-research before dispatch: specific vessel names/flags (current detail is aggregator-grade LOW-MED), any "JWC/Lloyd's reclassified Bab al-Mandab" claim (NOT yet observed), and any "Red Sea fully closed" extreme-absolute. Route Bab al-Mandab signals cluster IRAN_HORMUZ with BRENT action (BRT-28 fired on the activation side; BRENT 6/9 STATUS still says "Houthi quiet, rhetorical" = STALE, flag at next BRENT-routed dispatch).
- **Damage-vs-salvo regime read (HAWK 6/8) is routing-relevant.** Salvo cadence up, damage realization down, defenses holding every published wave. Signals framing raw salvo counts as escalation-equivalent get a precision overlay: the question is leakage (one missile through onto a hard target / first defended-asset loss with casualties), not count. The Apache downing is the closest leakage-class event so far (asset lost, crew survived). First leakage event with casualties = IMMEDIATE minimum regardless of other framing.
- **Blockade-enforcement signals gain a casualty/diplomatic-friction layer.** MT Settebello (1 dead, Indian crew, India formal protest) opens a third-country-friction vector — route India/flag-state/crew-nationality reaction signals as IRAN_HORMUZ with cluster_secondary per content; a second enforcement-casualty incident or an India policy response (shipping advisory, naval escort) = state-change, not color.
- **Pakistan-Munir mediator state: STILL the disambiguating missing data point.** No public Munir/Pakistan-MFA response to the suspension through 6/10; channel structurally alive (6/6 Haykal visit). A signal surfacing Munir's posture remains load-bearing — it disambiguates whether the residual channel is real or vestigial.
- **HAWK weights: REFRESHED 6/8 (C 53 / D 35 / B 12, convergence 31/45) — the "DOUBLY-STALE" flag is retired.** Caveat for scenario-pricing signals: 6/8 weights pre-date the overnight 6/9-10 three-country wave + Trump flip; D-Reescalation is likely understated until HAWK's next mark. Cite the weights WITH the pre-overnight caveat.
- **Hormuz throughput: UKMTO 7-day-avg is the canonical metric** (1.1/day w/e 6/3 = -97.8% vs pre-war 49; trajectory April 3.9 → May 2.8 → 1.1). "Effectively-total blockade with permitted-trickle exceptions" (HAWK 6/8) replaces "selective carve-outs." US Energy Secretary Wright's 6/10 "traffic rising" claim is unverified single-secondhand — if a signal leans on it, verify-research mandatory.
- **Tape-vs-narrative watch (updated):** the 6/9 crash → 6/10 rebound whipsaw shows the market repricing track-by-track. Intraday Brent attribution is unreliable in this regime — verify event-attribution (which track moved) before routing tape-move signals. Brent re-entering the $95-110 noise floor or breaking below $90 sustained are the CARL-relevant boundaries per ROUTING_TABLE Iran-cluster override (≤$95 sustained 5 sessions: 6/9 was day 1 below, 6/10 rebounded above — sustain counter reset).

Signals dispatched 6/10+ that lean on the fork (observer-dependent channel state) need re-framing to "fork resolved toward breakdown, residual channels"; signals reading the 6/8 mutual halt as war-wide de-escalation need the track-separation overlay (the Iran-US track escalated THROUGH the Iran-Israel halt).

## Superseded implications (as of Jun 2 — retained for diff)

- **Frame is now NARRATIVE-FORK + KINETIC-ACCELERATION** — not "paper-thaw + kinetic-relapse bifurcation" anymore. Paper-channel publicly fractured 6/1 but the resolution is dual: **Tasnim/IRGC says suspended; Araghchi MFA says ongoing; Trump Truth Social says continuing "rapid pace."** No single ground truth on bilateral status — **channel state is observer-dependent.** Kinetic-channel has accelerated to 4 distinct exchanges + 1 major Israeli ground push in 6 days (vs ~1/week prior cadence); **Kuwait strike cadence is the load-bearing escalation metric** (5/27 + 5/31→6/1 = SECOND Iran ballistic at Kuwait in 4 days). **Route Iran-cluster signals with this fork in mind: track WHICH narrative side AND whether kinetic-cadence-rate is the dominant tell.**
- **Trump rhetoric is tape-not-info IN BOTH DIRECTIONS now** — pattern symmetry. 5/23 "largely negotiated" was real-but-overstated; 6/1 "rapid pace" denial is also tape-not-info (containment signal toward market, not channel-state evidence). Iran cited this as MOU-suspension trigger (operationally proportionate). **Trump-brokered 5/31-6/1 Israel+Hezbollah halt-of-attacks announcement** sits as partial overlay but does not resolve the occupation-overhang. **Treat Israel-Lebanon ops as a direct Iran-cluster INPUT** — IDF posture / occupation extent / Hezbollah response are now first-order Iran-track variables, not background. - **Pakistan-Munir mediator state UNKNOWN as of 6/2.** No public response from Munir / Pakistan MFA to Tasnim suspension. Mediator-track status = missing data point. ## 🌊 2026-07-19 RED SEA / BAB EL-MANDEB LEG — folded in (Will-directed), WITH a year-conflation self-correction. Verified via one independent WALTER kinetic verify-agent (web sweep 7/15→7/19, briefed for explicit negatives + EXECUTED-vs-THREATENED) + WALTER's own confirming primary-search (The National / Hellenic Shipping News / gCaptain / CNBC / FDD / Security Council Report / MARAD). **This closes the standing "the anchor carries no Red Sea leg" hole — and it turns out the leg is a THREAT leg, not an executed-campaign leg.**

**🚩 THE HEADLINE FINDING — the "executed Houthi Red Sea campaign" the 7/17 addendum §5 folded in was YEAR-CONFLATED, and folding it honestly means RETRACTING it.** Search engines heavily conflate July 2024 / 2025 / 2026 Red Sea events, and the inbound batch that seeded §5 inherited that. **Independently confirmed:** the **Chios Lion** (Liberian crude tanker, Russian oil ex-Tuapse, USV hit, **200-220km oil slick**) = **15 July 2024**; the **Maersk Sentosa 7/9 explosion** = **9 July 2024**; the **Magic Seas / Eternity C sinkings** (the "vessels sunk / seafarers killed" imagery) = **early July 2025.** **None are 2026 events → retracted from the 2026 record.**

**WHAT IS ACTUALLY VERIFIABLE IN 2026 (executed) — minor, and NOT a sustained campaign:**
- **6/10/2026** — armed guards on a cargo vessel **repelled a small-boat attack** near Bab al-Mandeb (post the June-8 Houthi threat to resume). No damage.
- **7/5/2026** — an **UNCLAIMED** skiff + small-arms attack ~30nm SW of Al Hudaydah; security team returned fire, skiff withdrew to a mothership. **No damage, no casualties, no group claimed it** (could be piracy — Houthi attribution NOT established). [UKMTO / Bloomberg / CNBC 7/5]
- **✅ LOAD-BEARING NEGATIVES (2026):** **NO vessel confirmed SUNK by hostile action in the Red Sea in 2026; NO confirmed 2026 crew deaths.** The Security Council July-2026 forecast + Wikipedia both state the Houthis had **not resumed sustained maritime attacks** in 2026 beyond the 7/5 unclaimed event. *(Caveat: those two sources may pre-date the mid-July war re-escalation; but WALTER's own 7/19 search found no resumed maritime attack through 7/19 either — MEDIUM-HIGH on the negative, from absence-of-reporting.)*

**THE GENUINELY-LIVE LEG IS THREAT / CONDITIONAL, not executed:**
- **The Bab el-Mandeb CONDITIONAL (= ladder 3b, well-sourced):** Iran asked the Houthis to **STAND READY to close the Red Sea oil route IF the US strikes Iran's power grid** [Reuters exclusive, 3 sources, ~7/16; corroborated FDD 7/17]. **NOT executed — no order given; strait OPEN; IRGC reps in Yemen hold the timing.** Reported drone/missile positioning near Bab el-Mandeb is **single-sourced** (one Houthi-proximate source) → CLAIMED, not verified. **Couples directly to Trump's 7/20-26 grid-strike threat** — this is the two-sided trigger.
- **Standing Houthi threat posture:** the group threatened (June 8) to resume targeting Israeli-linked ships; the IRGC has framed a **"security belt from Hormuz to Bab el-Mandeb"** as the retaliation geometry. Rhetoric, not execution.

**OIL-CHOKEPOINT CONTEXT (why the leg matters even unexecuted):** **~7% of global oil transited Bab el-Mandeb in June 2026** [Kpler via Reuters 7/16]; southern-Red-Sea transit already runs **~30-35 vessels/day vs a ~70/day pre-crisis baseline** (roughly halved). **With Hormuz already constrained, a Bab el-Mandeb closure would hit BOTH main Middle-East oil-export routes at once** — and Saudi Arabia's **Yanbu / East-West-pipeline bypass (up to ~5M bpd)** routes crude northbound *through* the Red Sea, so a Red Sea closure specifically degrades the Hormuz bypass. *(Transit granularity is coarse — no clean Hormuz-style daily count for this exact week.)*

### ① US–IRAN — 🔴 **BOTH SIDES NOW DEMAND COMPENSATION FROM THE OTHER AS A PRECONDITION. That is a SYMMETRIC blocker and it is new since the 8/07 banner.**

- **Iran (Araghchi):** Iran and Oman are **"very close"** on a deal to manage the Strait; Oman's Foreign Ministry calls the talks **"positive and constructive."** But reopening remains **"subject to other conditions," including COMPENSATION FROM THE US.**
- **Trump, 8/10:** would now **require compensation FROM IRAN** for past misdeeds **as a condition for talks** — applied *after* Tehran said US compensation would be required to reopen the Strait.
- **Trump, Sun 8/09:** the US is **"semi-negotiating"** with Iran, **while Iranian state media said no formal negotiations were underway.**

**🔑 THE READ: the demand is now RECIPROCAL AND SYMMETRIC.** Each side has made the other's compensation a precondition, which is a structurally harder blocker than a one-sided ask — there is no longer an obvious party who can concede first without it reading as capitulation. **The reporting's own summary: the back-and-forth left the two sides "no closer" to an agreement that would reopen the Strait.**

**🔑 AND THE TWO TRACKS HAVE DECOUPLED — this is the cleanest instance yet of this file's own mediated-≠-bilateral guard.** **Iran↔OMAN is converging** ("very close," "positive and constructive"). **Iran↔US has gone BACKWARD** ("semi-negotiating" vs "no formal negotiations," plus a new precondition). ⇒ **Progress on the Omani track is NOT progress on the American one, and a headline about "the Hormuz deal" advancing can be true of the first while the second deteriorates.** Anyone pricing a reopening off Oman-track optimism is pricing the wrong track.

⚠️ **SOURCING LIMIT, STATED: the 8/08 and 8/10 items are from SEARCH-RESULT SNIPPETS of CNN live-blogs, NOT fetched articles — `cnn.com` returns HTTP 451 to this box** (the same block ADDENDUM #18 recorded). **Treat the quoted conditions as reported-not-primary-verified.** The Araghchi/Oman-FM characterisations are corroborated across the snippet set; the exact wording is not.
